# Agents · S · part 6

1,047 entries, scope-architect to scout. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **scope-architect**: Tu es l'agent de scoping de Forge. Ton travail consiste à prendre un PRD validé et à le transformer en une roadmap cohérente qui évite le piège classique du projet trop ambitieux qui n'aboutit jamais. Tu ne redéfinis pas les besoins produit — tu décides de **quand** et **dans quel ordre** ils seront adressés. - **scope-audit**: SCOPE audit orchestrator — single entry point for the full audit pipeline. - **scope-auditor**: Decides what an INTERMEDIATE dasLLAMA pull request must run before it merges - the scope report (skills/internal/dasllama_testing.md). Give it the diff range, the boxes at hand and the runs already made on the branch; it returns three lists - RUN (exact commands), NOT RUN (with reasons) and RISK - and runs nothing. Its bias is a SHORT run list - iteration speed is what an intermediate PR buys, and the arc's final PR runs everything. ONE instance a PR, before any model-loading run starts; never for a final PR, whose run list is the run rules' own. Note - a NEW definition file hot-loads mid-ses… ([GaijinEntertainment/daScript](https://github.com/GaijinEntertainment/daScript/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scope-auditor.md) · ★1.2k)

- **scope-auditor**: Adversarial governance reviewer (the CPO's proxy). Reviews the cumulative branch diff against the task contract and the decision-rights table before any commit. Read-only. Invoked in step 2 (Blinding) of the review cycle — never for building. ([ramialfahham/football-data-pipeline](https://github.com/ramialfahham/football-data-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scope-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **scope-auditor**: Independent blast-radius auditor for a supply-chain action. Use before a consequential action to check that the proposed scope matches the evidence - the domain's most dangerous error. It sizes BOTH the under-scope risk (missed affected items) and the over-scope risk (touching unaffected items), runs the population tie-out, and checks cumulative/salami exposure. Returns a scope verdict; never widens or narrows on its own, never executes. ([MetaFloor-AI/metafloor-scm-plugin](https://github.com/MetaFloor-AI/metafloor-scm-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/scope-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **scope-auditor**: Post-dispatch scope/contamination GATE. Given a dispatched branch + its declared expected_files, diffs it against fresh origin/main, classifies every surplus file as acceptable collateral vs contamination, and prescribes the mandatory remediation (re-integrate by cherry-picking the one clean commit — never rebase, never salvage-in-place). Use on every dispatched-branch return before merge. ([Roeelfs/keel](https://github.com/Roeelfs/keel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scope-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **scope-auditor**: Read-only Plan→Execute boundary check. For every commit that changes a public function/component/type signature, traces every caller-site outside the commit's owned-files, then either confirms `internal_only: true` or appends the missing caller files to the appropriate commit's owned-files list. Prevents the "fan-out scope-blindness" defect class observed in round-2 of dispatch-pattern testing (example-app 2026-05-07). ([tyroneross/build-loop](https://github.com/tyroneross/build-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/scope-auditor.md) · ★1)

- **scope-checker**: Tests whether the proposed solution is proportional to the actual scale of the problem. Addresses Scope Neglect (62), Additive Bias (57), Quantification Bias (73), and Illusion of Validity (46). Uses order-of-magnitude perturbation: if the problem were 10x larger or 10x smaller, would the solution scale appropriately? Also checks for subtraction-blindness — solutions that add complexity when removing something would be better. Lightweight agent, activated on STANDARD and FULL protocols when solutions involve resource allocation, budget, team size, or timeline. ([jamon8888/cc-suite](https://github.com/jamon8888/cc-suite/blob/HEAD/sentinel/agents/scope-checker.md))
- **scope-checker**: Compares diff against branch name and commit messages to detect scope creep or missing parts. Used by the review plugin orchestrator for diffs over 500 lines. ([phyr97/review](https://github.com/phyr97/review/blob/HEAD/agents/scope-checker.md))
- **scope-checker**: Use this agent to verify a branch's diff stays within the scope declared by its ticket. Enforces CLAUDE.md Rule 5 ("Bug fix ≠ refactor") and Rule 6 ("One ticket per branch"). - **scope-editor**: ProductForge scope editor.
